\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \stackrel {(4 0)} {\leq} v _ {k - 1} - \frac {2 \gamma \mu}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} + \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \\ + \frac {2 \gamma}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k - 1}) - \nabla f (x ^ {k - 1}) \right\rangle \\ \stackrel {(1 3 2)} {\leq} \quad (1 - 2 \gamma \mu) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \\ + \frac {2 \gamma}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(\frac {\mu}{2} \| x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 \mu} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k - 1}) - \nabla f (x ^ {k - 1}) \| ^ {2}\right) \\ \stackrel {(1 5)} {\leq} (1 - \gamma \mu) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] + \frac {\gamma \zeta^ {2}}{\mu}. \\ \end{array}
$$

taking full expectation on both sides of previous inequality, we obtain

$$
\mathbf {e} v _ {k} \stackrel {(1 4 0)} {\leq} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k - 1} \right] + \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \right] + \frac {\gamma \zeta^ {2}}{\mu}.
$$

let  $t$  be a non-negative integer for which  $\tau t \leq k < \tau (t + 1)$ . using this and  $v_{\tau t} = 0$ , we unroll the recurrence and derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} {\bf e} [ v _ {k} ] \leq \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {l = \tau t} ^ {k - 1} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} {\bf e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {l} \| ^ {2} \right] + \frac {\gamma \zeta^ {2} (k - \tau t)}{\mu} \\ \stackrel {(8)} {\leq} \gamma^ {2} \sum_ {l = \tau t} ^ {k - 1} \left(2 a \mathbf {e} \left[ f (x ^ {l}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] + b \mathbf {e} [ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} ] + f \mathbf {e} [ v _ {l} ] + d _ {1}\right) + \frac {\gamma \zeta^ {2} (k - \tau t)}{\mu}, \\ \end{array}
$$

whence

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {j = \tau t} ^ {k} w _ {j} \mathbf {e} v _ {j} \leq 2 a \gamma^ {2} \sum_ {j = \tau t} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = \tau t} ^ {j - 1} w _ {j} \mathbf {e} \left[ f (x ^ {l}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] + b \gamma^ {2} \sum_ {j = \tau t} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = \tau t} ^ {j - 1} w _ {j} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \\ + f \gamma^ {2} \sum_ {j = \tau t} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = \tau t} ^ {j - 1} w _ {j} \mathbf {e} v _ {l} + (\tau - 1) \left(\gamma^ {2} d _ {1} + \frac {\gamma \zeta^ {2}}{\mu}\right) \sum_ {j = \tau t} ^ {k} w _ {j}. \\ \end{array}
$$

if we substitute  $a$  with  $e(\widetilde{a} (\tau -1) + \widehat{a})$ ,  $b$  with  $e(\widetilde{b} (\tau -1) + \widehat{b})$ ,  $f$  with  $e(\widetilde{f} (\tau -1) + \widehat{f})$ , and  $\left(\gamma^2 d_1 + \frac{\gamma\zeta^2}{\mu}\right)$  with  $e\gamma^{2}(\widetilde{d}_{1}(\tau -1) + \widehat{d}_{1})$  in the inequality above, we will get inequality (29). following the same steps as in the proof of lemma e.1, we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} v _ {k} \leq 4 (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right) \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {2 b \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} (2 + \rho) (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2}}{\rho} \\ + 2 (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \left(f + \frac {2 b g}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right) \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} {\bf e} v _ {k} + (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {2 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right) w _ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

our choice of  $\gamma$  implies that

$$
4 (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right) \leq \frac {1}{8 l} \quad \mathrm {a n d} \quad 2 (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \left(f + \frac {2 b g}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right) \leq \frac {1}{2}.
$$

using these inequalities we continue our derivations

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{2} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} v _ {k} \leq \frac {1}{8 l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {2 b \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} (2 + \rho) (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2}}{\rho} \\ + (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {2 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right) w _ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

multiplying both sides by  $4l$  we get the result.

![](images/ee97a1e3347c3d1ba8e3f8cdf799f7f2872a2946bcd98ffbb28129fa629850b6.jpg)

clearly, this lemma and theorem 2.1 imply the following result.

corollary e.2. let the assumptions of lemma e.2 be satisfied. then assumption 2.3 holds and, in particular, if

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma \leq \min  \left\{\frac {1}{2 (a ^ {\prime} + c m)}, \frac {l}{f ^ {\prime} + g m} \right\}, \quad m = \frac {4 b ^ {\prime}}{3 \rho}, \\ \gamma \leq \min \left\{\frac {1}{4 (\tau - 1) \mu}, \frac {1}{2 \sqrt {(\tau - 1) \left(f + \frac {2 b g}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {2 l (\tau - 1) \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}} \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

then for all  $k \geq 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \frac {2 t ^ {0} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + m d _ {2} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right), \tag {42}
$$

where  $\overline{x}^k \stackrel{\text{def}}{=} \frac{1}{w_k} \sum_{k=0}^{k} w_k x^k$  and

$$
h = \frac {4 b (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} (2 + \rho)}{\rho}, d _ {3} = 2 (\tau - 1) \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {2 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right).
$$

moreover, if  $\mu > 0$ , then

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \left(1 - \min  \left\{\gamma \mu , \frac {\rho}{4} \right\}\right) ^ {k} \frac {2 t ^ {0} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + m d _ {2} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right), \tag {43}
$$

and in the case when  $\mu = 0$ , we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \frac {2 t ^ {0} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + m d _ {2} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right). \tag {44}
$$

# e.2 random local loop

in this section we show how our results can be applied to analyze (4) in the case when

$$
c _ {k} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} 1, & \text {w i t h p r o b a b i l i t y} p, \\ 0, & \text {w i t h p r o b a b i l i t y} 1 - p, \end{array} \right.
$$

where  $p$  encodes the probability of initiating communication. this choice in effect leads to a method using a random-length local loop on all devices.

# e.2.1 heterogeneous data

as in section e.1.1, our analysis of (4) with random length of the local loop relies on assumption e.1. next lemma provides an upper bound for the weighted sum of  $\mathbf{e}[v_k]$  in this case.

lemma e.3. let assumptions 2.1, 2.2 and  $e.1$  be satisfied and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma \leq \min  \left\{\frac {p}{1 6 \mu}, \frac {p}{2 \sqrt {(1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {f} + p \widehat {f})}} \right\}, \\ \gamma \leq \min \left\{\frac {p \sqrt {3 \rho (1 - \rho)}}{8 \sqrt {2 g (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}}, \frac {p \sqrt {3}}{1 6 \sqrt {2 l (1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {a} + p \widehat {a} + \frac {2 c ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{\rho (1 - \rho)})}} \right\}. \\ \end{array}
$$

then (11) holds with

$$
h = \frac {6 4 (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2} \rho}, \quad d _ {3} = \frac {8 (1 - p)}{p ^ {2}} \left((p + 2) \widetilde {d} _ {1} + p \widehat {d} _ {1} + \frac {8 d _ {2} ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{3 \rho}\right). \tag {45}
$$

proof. first of all, we introduce new notation:  $\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x^k,g^k ]\stackrel {\mathrm{def}}{=}\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x_1^k,\ldots ,x_n^k,g_1^k,\ldots ,g_n^k],\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x^k ]\stackrel {\mathrm{def}}{=}\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid$ $x_{1}^{k},\dots ,x_{n}^{k}]$  . by definition of  $v_{k}$  , we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k + 1} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \stackrel {(1 4 0)} {=} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \mathbf {e} \left[ \| x _ {i} ^ {k + 1} - x ^ {k + 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k}, g ^ {k} \right] \mid x ^ {k} \right]
$$

$$
= \frac {1 - p}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} - \gamma g _ {i} ^ {k} + \gamma g ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right]
$$

$$
\stackrel {(1 3 9)} {=} \frac {1 - p}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} - \gamma \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} + \gamma \bar {g} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} - (g ^ {k} - \bar {g} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \stackrel {(1 3 5), (1 3 9)} {\leq} \frac {(1 - p) \left(1 + \frac {p}{2}\right)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} + \frac {(1 - p) \left(1 + \frac {2}{p}\right) \gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \\ + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\stackrel {(1 3 8), (1 3 9)} {\leq} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) v _ {k} + \frac {(1 - p) (2 + p) \gamma^ {2}}{p n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\| \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right],
$$

where  $\bar{g}^k = \mathbf{e}[g^k\mid x^k]$ . taking the full expectation we derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k + 1} \right] \leq \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] + \frac {(1 - p) (2 + p) \gamma^ {2}}{p n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \right] + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \right] \\ \stackrel {(2 6), (2 7)} {\leq} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] + 2 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {a} + \widehat {a}\right) \mathbf {e} \left[ f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \\ + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {b} + \widehat {b}\right) {\bf e} \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} + \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {f} + \widehat {f}\right) {\bf e} v _ {k}\right) \\ + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {d} _ {1} + \widehat {d} _ {1}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

this inequality together with  $\gamma \leq \frac{p}{2\sqrt{(1 - p)((2 + p)\widetilde{f} + p\widehat{f})}}$  imply

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k + 1} \right] \leq \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] + 2 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {a} + \widehat {a}\right) \mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \\ + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {b} + \widehat {b}\right) \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {d} _ {1} + \widehat {d} _ {1}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

unrolling the recurrence, we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k + 1} \right] \leq 2 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {a} + \widehat {a}\right) \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} \mathbf {e} \left[ f (x ^ {l}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \\ + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {b} + \widehat {b}\right) \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \\ + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(\frac {2 + p}{p} \widetilde {d} _ {1} + \widehat {d} _ {1}\right) \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l}. \\ \end{array}
$$

as a consequence, we derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} ] \leq \frac {2 (1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {a} + p \widehat {a}) \gamma^ {2}}{p (1 - \frac {p}{4})} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} r _ {l} \\ + \frac {(1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}) \gamma^ {2}}{p (1 - \frac {p}{4})} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} ] \\ + \frac {(1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {d} _ {1} + p \widehat {d} _ {1}) \gamma^ {2}}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - 1 - l} w _ {k}, \tag {46} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we use new notation:  $r_l = \mathbf{e}\left[f(x^l) - f(x^*)\right]$ . recall that  $w_k = (1 - \eta)^{-(k + 1)}$  and  $\eta = \min \left\{\gamma \mu, \frac{\rho}{4}\right\}$ . together with our assumption on  $\gamma$  it implies that for all  $0 \leq i < k$  we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} w _ {k} \quad = \quad (1 - \eta) ^ {- (k - i + 1)} \left(1 - \eta\right) ^ {- i} \stackrel {(1 3 7)} {\leq} w _ {k - i} \left(1 + 2 \eta\right) ^ {i} \\ \leq w _ {k - i} \left(1 + 2 \gamma \mu\right) ^ {i} \leq w _ {k - i} \left(1 + \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {i}, \tag {47} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
w _ {k} = (1 - \eta) ^ {- (k - i + 1)} (1 - \eta) ^ {- i} \stackrel {(1 3 7)} {\leq} w _ {k - i} (1 + 2 \eta) ^ {i} \leq w _ {k - i} \left(1 + \frac {\rho}{2}\right) ^ {i}, \tag {48}
$$

$$
w _ {k} \stackrel {(1 3 7)} {\leq} (1 + 2 \eta) ^ {k + 1} \leq \left(1 + \frac {\rho}{2}\right) ^ {k + 1}. \tag {49}
$$

having these inequalities in hand we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} r _ {l} \quad \stackrel {(4 7)} {\leq} \quad \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} \left(1 + \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} r _ {l} \\ \stackrel {(1 3 8)} {\leq} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} r _ {l} \leq \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k}\right) \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {k}\right) \\ = \frac {8}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \quad \stackrel {(4 7)} {\leq} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} \left(1 + \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \\ \stackrel {(1 3 8)} {\leq} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \leq \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right]\right) \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {p}{8}\right) ^ {k}\right) \\ = \quad \frac {8}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - 1 - l} w _ {k} \leq \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k}\right) \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k}\right) = \frac {4 w _ {k}}{p}.
$$

plugging these inequalities together with  $1 - \frac{p}{4} \geq \frac{3}{4}$  in (46), we derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq \frac {6 4 (1 - p) \left((2 + p) \widetilde {a} + p \widehat {a}\right) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2}} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {3 2 (1 - p) \left((2 + p) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}\right) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2}} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right] \\ + \frac {4 (1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {d} _ {1} + p \widehat {d} _ {1}) \gamma^ {2}}{p ^ {2}} w _ {k}. \tag {50} \\ \end{array}
$$

it remains to estimate the second term on the right-hand side of this inequality. we notice that an analogous term appears in the proof of lemma e.1. in particular, in that proof inequality (35) was shown via inequalities (10), (48), (49) and (138) which hold in this case too. therefore, we get that

$$
\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right] \stackrel {(3 5)} {\leq} \frac {\mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} (2 + \rho)}{\rho} + \frac {4 c}{\rho (1 - \rho)} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {2 g}{\rho (1 - \rho)} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} v _ {k} + \frac {d _ {2} w _ {k}}{\rho},
$$

whence

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \stackrel {(5 0)} {\leq} \frac {6 4 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left((2 + p) \widetilde {a} + p \widehat {a} + \frac {2 c \left((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}\right)}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}{3 p ^ {2}} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} \\ + \frac {3 2 (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2} \rho} \\ + \frac {6 4 g (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2} \rho (1 - \rho)} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} ] \\ + \frac {4 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p ^ {2}} \left((p + 2) \widetilde {d} _ {1} + p \widehat {d} _ {1} + \frac {8 d _ {2} ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{3 \rho}\right) w _ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

our assumptions on  $\gamma$  imply

$$
\frac {6 4 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left((2 + p) \widetilde {a} + p \widehat {a} + \frac {2 c ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}{3 p ^ {2}} \leq \frac {1}{8 l}, \quad \frac {6 4 g (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2} \rho (1 - \rho)} \leq \frac {1}{2}.
$$

next, we introduce new notation as follows:

$$
h = \frac {6 4 (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2} \rho}, d _ {3} = \frac {8 (1 - p)}{p ^ {2}} \left((p + 2) \widetilde {d} _ {1} + p \widehat {d} _ {1} + \frac {8 d _ {2} ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{3 \rho}\right).
$$

putting all together, we get

$$
\frac {1}{2} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} ] \leq \frac {1}{8 l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {h}{2} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {d _ {3}}{2} \gamma^ {2} w _ {k},
$$

which concludes the proof.

this lemma and theorem 2.1 imply the following result.

corollary e.3. let the assumptions of lemma e.3 be satisfied. then assumption 2.3 holds and, in particular, if

$$
\begin{array}{l} \gamma \leq \min \left\{\frac {1}{2 \left(a ^ {\prime} + \frac {4 b ^ {\prime} c}{3 \rho}\right)}, \frac {l}{f ^ {\prime} + \frac {4 b ^ {\prime} g}{3 \rho}}, \frac {p}{1 6 \mu}, \frac {p}{2 \sqrt {(1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {f} + p \widehat {f})}} \right\}, \\ \gamma \leq \min \left\{\frac {p \sqrt {3 \rho (1 - \rho)}}{8 \sqrt {2 g (1 - p) ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}}, \frac {p \sqrt {3}}{1 6 \sqrt {2 l (1 - p) ((2 + p) \widetilde {a} + p \widehat {a} + \frac {2 c ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{\rho (1 - \rho)})}} \right\}, \\ \end{array}
$$

then for all  $k \geq 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} + \frac {8 b ^ {\prime}}{3 \rho} \gamma^ {2} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \frac {4 b ^ {\prime} d _ {2}}{3 \rho} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right), \tag {51}
$$

where  $\overline{x}^k \stackrel{\text{def}}{=} \frac{1}{w_k} \sum_{k=0}^{k} w_k x^k$  and

$$
h = \frac {6 4 (1 - p) \left((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b}\right) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2}}{3 p ^ {2} \rho}, \quad d _ {3} = \frac {8 (1 - p)}{p ^ {2}} \left((p + 2) \widetilde {d} _ {1} + p \widehat {d} _ {1} + \frac {8 d _ {2} ((p + 2) \widetilde {b} + p \widehat {b})}{3 \rho}\right).
$$

moreover, if  $\mu > 0$ , then

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \left(1 - \min \left\{\gamma \mu , \frac {\rho}{4} \right\}\right) ^ {k} \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} + \frac {8 b ^ {\prime}}{3 \rho} \gamma^ {2} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma} \\ + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \frac {4 b ^ {\prime} d _ {2}}{3 \rho} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right), \tag {52} \\ \end{array}
$$

and in the case when  $\mu = 0$ , we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} + \frac {8 b ^ {\prime}}{3 \rho} \gamma^ {2} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \frac {4 b ^ {\prime} d _ {2}}{3 \rho} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right). \quad (5 3)
$$

# e.2.2  $\zeta$ -heterogeneous data

in this section we assume that  $f_{1},f_{2},\ldots ,f_{n}$  are  $\zeta$ -heterogeneous (see definition 3.1). moreover, we additionally assume that  $\mathbf{e}\left[g_i^k\mid x_i^k\right] = \nabla f_i(x_i^k)$  and we also assume  $\mu$ -strong convexity of the functions  $f_{i}$  for  $i\in [n]$ .

lemma e.4. let assumption 2.2 be satisfied, inequalities (7)-(10) hold and<sup>18</sup>

$$
\gamma \leq \min  \left\{\frac {p}{8 \mu}, \sqrt {\frac {p}{2 f (1 - p)}}, \sqrt {\frac {p \rho (1 - \rho)}{3 2 b g (1 - p)}}, \sqrt {\frac {p}{1 2 8 l (1 - p) \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}} \right\}.
$$

moreover, assume that  $f_{1}, f_{2}, \ldots, f_{n}$  are  $\zeta$ -heterogeneous and  $\mu$ -strongly convex, and  $\mathbf{e}\left[g_i^k \mid x_i^k\right] = \nabla f_i(x_i^k)$  for all  $i \in [n]$ . then (11) holds with

$$
h = \frac {1 6 b (1 - p) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2}}{p \rho}, \quad d _ {3} = \frac {4 (1 - p)}{p} \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {4 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right). \tag {54}
$$

proof. first of all, we introduce new notation:  $\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x^k,g^k ]\stackrel {\mathrm{def}}{=}\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x_1^k,\ldots ,x_n^k,g_1^k,\ldots ,g_n^k ]$ . by definition of  $v_{k}$  for all  $k\geq 1$  we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} \mid x ^ {k - 1}, g ^ {k - 1} ] \stackrel {(4), (2 0)} {=} \frac {1 - p}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\| x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - x ^ {k - 1} - \gamma g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} + \gamma g ^ {k - 1} \right\| ^ {2} \\ = \quad \frac {1 - p}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - x ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - x ^ {k - 1}, g ^ {k - 1} - g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \right\rangle \\ + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - g ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \\ = \quad (1 - p) v _ {k - 1} + 2 \gamma (1 - p) \left\langle \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - x ^ {k - 1}, g ^ {k - 1} \right\rangle \\ + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \right\rangle + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - g ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \\ = (1 - p) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \right\rangle \\ + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - g ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

next, we take the conditional expectation  $\mathbf{e}\left[\cdot \mid x^{k - 1}\right] \stackrel{\mathrm{def}}{=} \mathbf{e}\left[\cdot \mid x_1^{k - 1},\ldots ,x_n^{k - 1}\right]$  on both sides of the obtained inequality and get

$$
\begin{array}{l} {\mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right]} = {(1 - p) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}) \right\rangle} \\ + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} - g ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \\ \stackrel {(1 3 9)} {\leq} (1 - p) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}\right) - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {k - 1}\right) \right\rangle \\ + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {k - 1}\right) \right\rangle \\ + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right]. \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i = 1}^{n}\langle x^{k - 1} - x_i^{k - 1},\nabla f(x^{k - 1})\rangle = 0$ , we can continue as follows:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \overset {(4 0)} {\leq} (1 - p) v _ {k - 1} - \frac {2 \gamma \mu (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \\ + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\langle x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1}, \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k - 1}) - \nabla f (x ^ {k - 1}) \right\rangle \\ + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \stackrel {(1 3 2)} {\leq} (1 - p) (1 - 2 \gamma \mu) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] \\ + \frac {2 \gamma (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(\frac {\mu}{2} \| x ^ {k - 1} - x _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 \mu} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k - 1}) - \nabla f (x ^ {k - 1}) \| ^ {2}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\stackrel {(1 5)} {\leq} (1 - p) (1 - \gamma \mu) v _ {k - 1} + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k - 1} \right] + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma \zeta^ {2}}{\mu}.
$$

taking full mathematical expectation on both sides of previous inequality and using  $1 - \gamma \mu \leq 1$  we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} v _ {k} \stackrel {(1 4 0)} {\leq} (1 - p) \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k - 1} ] + \frac {\gamma^ {2} (1 - p)}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} [ \| g _ {i} ^ {k - 1} \| ^ {2} ] + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma \zeta^ {2}}{\mu} \\ \stackrel {(8)} {\leq} (1 - p) \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k - 1} ] + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(2 a \mathbf {e} [ f (x ^ {k - 1}) - f (x ^ {*}) ] + b \mathbf {e} [ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} ] + f \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k - 1} ] + d _ {1}\right) \\ + \frac {(1 - p) \gamma \zeta^ {2}}{\mu}. \\ \end{array}
$$

since  $\gamma \leq \sqrt{\frac{p}{2f(1 - p)}}$  we have  $(1 - p)\gamma^{2}f\leq \frac{p}{2}$  and

$$
{\bf e} v _ {k} \leq \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) {\bf e} [ v _ {k - 1} ] + (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(2 a {\bf e} [ f (x ^ {k - 1}) - f (x ^ {*}) ] + b {\bf e} [ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} ] + d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu}\right).
$$

unrolling the recurrence we obtain

$$
{\bf e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - 1 - l} \left(2 a {\bf e} \left[ f (x ^ {l}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] + b {\bf e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] + d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu}\right).
$$

as a consequence, we derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} ] \leq \frac {2 a (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{1 - \frac {p}{2}} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} r _ {l} \\ + \frac {b (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{1 - \frac {p}{2}} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \\ + \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu}\right) (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - 1 - l} w _ {k}, \tag {55} \\ \end{array}
$$

where we use new notation:  $r_l = \mathbf{e}\left[f(x^l) - f(x^*)\right]$ . recall that  $w_k = (1 - \eta)^{-(k + 1)}$  and  $\eta = \min \left\{\gamma \mu, \frac{\rho}{4}\right\}$ .

together with our assumption on  $\gamma$  it implies that for all  $0\leq i < k$  we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} w _ {k} \quad = \quad (1 - \eta) ^ {- (k - i + 1)} (1 - \eta) ^ {- i} \stackrel {(1 3 7)} {\leq} w _ {k - i} (1 + 2 \eta) ^ {i} \\ \leq \quad w _ {k - i} (1 + 2 \gamma \mu) ^ {i} \leq w _ {k - i} \left(1 + \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {i}, \tag {56} \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
w _ {k} = (1 - \eta) ^ {- (k - i + 1)} (1 - \eta) ^ {- i} \stackrel {(1 3 7)} {\leq} w _ {k - i} (1 + 2 \eta) ^ {i} \leq w _ {k - i} \left(1 + \frac {\rho}{2}\right) ^ {i}, \tag {57}
$$

$$
w _ {k} \stackrel {(1 3 7)} {\leq} (1 + 2 \eta) ^ {k + 1} \leq \left(1 + \frac {\rho}{2}\right) ^ {k + 1}. \tag {58}
$$

having these inequalities in hand we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} r _ {l} \quad \stackrel {(5 6)} {\leq} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - l} \left(1 + \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} r _ {l} \\ \stackrel {(1 3 8)} {\leq} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} r _ {l} \leq \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k}\right) \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k}\right) \\ = \quad \frac {4}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k}, \\ \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \quad \stackrel {(5 6)} {\leq} \quad \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - l} \left(1 + \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \\ \stackrel {(1 3 8)} {\leq} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k - l} w _ {l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {l} ^ {2} \right] \leq \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right]\right) \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {p}{4}\right) ^ {k}\right) \\ = \quad \frac {4}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right], \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} \sum_ {l = 0} ^ {k - 1} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k - 1 - l} w _ {k} \leq \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k}\right) \left(\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {\infty} \left(1 - \frac {p}{2}\right) ^ {k}\right) = \frac {2 w _ {k}}{p}.
$$

plugging these inequalities together with  $1 - \frac{p}{2} \geq \frac{1}{2}$  in (55) we derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq \frac {1 6 a (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {8 b (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right] \\ + \frac {2 \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu}\right) (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p} w _ {k}. \tag {59} \\ \end{array}
$$

it remains to estimate the second term in the right-hand side of this inequality. we notice that an analogous term appears in the proof of lemma e.1. in particular, in that proof inequality (35) was shown via inequalities (10), (48), (49) and (138) which hold in this case too. therefore, we get that

$$
\sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \right] \stackrel {(3 5)} {\leq} \frac {\mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} (2 + \rho)}{\rho} + \frac {4 c}{\rho (1 - \rho)} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {2 g}{\rho (1 - \rho)} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} v _ {k} + \frac {d _ {2} w _ {k}}{\rho},
$$

hence

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} ] \stackrel {(5 0)} {\leq} \frac {1 6 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}{p} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} \\ + \frac {8 b (1 - p) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2} {\bf e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{p \rho} + \frac {1 6 b g (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p \rho (1 - \rho)} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} {\bf e} [ v _ {k} ] \\ + \frac {2 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p} \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {4 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right) w _ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

our assumption on  $\gamma$  imply

$$
\frac {1 6 (1 - p) \gamma^ {2} \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}{p} \leq \frac {1}{8 l}, \quad \frac {1 6 b g (1 - p) \gamma^ {2}}{p \rho (1 - \rho)} \leq \frac {1}{2}.
$$

next, we introduce new notation as follows:

$$
h = \frac {1 6 b (1 - p) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2}}{p \rho}, d _ {3} = \frac {4 (1 - p)}{p} \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {4 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right).
$$

putting all together we get

$$
\frac {1}{2} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} [ v _ {k} ] \leq \frac {1}{8 l} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} r _ {k} + \frac {h}{2} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2} + \frac {d _ {3}}{2} \gamma^ {2} w _ {k}
$$

which concludes the proof.

![](images/a6563303dc0842845c49856c6917bcda4aab50818f9b607602844cfa7718789c.jpg)

this lemma and theorem 2.1 imply the following result.

corollary e.4. let the assumptions of lemma e.4 are satisfied. then assumption 2.3 holds and, in particular, if

$$
\gamma \leq \min  \left\{\frac {1}{2 (a ^ {\prime} + c m)}, \frac {l}{f ^ {\prime} + g m}, \frac {p}{8 \mu} \right\}, \quad m = \frac {4 b ^ {\prime}}{3 \rho},
$$

$$
\gamma \leq \min \left\{\sqrt {\frac {p}{2 f (1 - p)}}, \sqrt {\frac {p \rho (1 - \rho)}{3 2 b g (1 - p)}}, \sqrt {\frac {p}{1 2 8 l (1 - p) \left(a + \frac {2 b c}{\rho (1 - \rho)}\right)}} \right\},
$$

then for all  $k \geq 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 t ^ {0} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + m d _ {2} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right), \tag {60}
$$

where  $\overline{x}^k \stackrel{\text{def}}{=} \frac{1}{w_k} \sum_{k=0}^{k} w_k x^k$  and

$$
h = \frac {1 6 b (1 - p) (2 + \rho) \gamma^ {2}}{p \rho}, d _ {3} = \frac {4 (1 - p)}{p} \left(d _ {1} + \frac {\zeta^ {2}}{\gamma \mu} + \frac {4 b d _ {2}}{\rho}\right).
$$

moreover, if  $\mu > 0$ , then

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \left(1 - \min \left\{\gamma \mu , \frac {\rho}{4} \right\}\right) ^ {k} \frac {2 t ^ {0} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma} \\ + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + m d _ {2} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right), \tag {61} \\ \end{array}
$$

and in the case when  $\mu = 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 t ^ {0} + 4 l h \gamma \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {0} ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(d _ {1} ^ {\prime} + m d _ {2} + 2 l \gamma d _ {3}\right). \tag {62}
$$

# f missing parts from section 4

let us start with an useful lemma that bounds the bregman distance between the local iterate  $x_{i}^{k}$  and the optimum  $x^{*}$  by the bregman distance between the virtual iterate  $x^{k}$  and the optimum.

lemma f.1. assume  $f_{i}$  is  $l$ -smooth for all  $i \in [n]$ . then

$$
d _ {f _ {i}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) \leq 2 d _ {f _ {i}} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + l \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \quad \forall i \in [ n ]. \tag {63}
$$

proof. using corollaries of  $l$ -smoothness and young's inequality, we derive

$$
\begin{array}{l} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) \stackrel {(1 8)} {\leq} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + \left\langle \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {k}\right) - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right), x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \right\rangle + \frac {l}{2} \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(1 3 2)} {\leq} d _ {f _ {i}} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + \frac {1}{2 l} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} + l \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6)} {\leq} 2 d _ {f _ {i}} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + l \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/7b88dd635d7ce15e922e046a21e2ad660fe12a4d3dc955288faa5da5e01f7457.jpg)

# f.1 proof of lemma 4.1

let us bound  $\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i = 1}^{n}\mathbf{e}_k\left[\| g_i^k\| ^2\right]$  first:

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \right] = \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \| a _ {i} ^ {k} - b _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \right] \\ = \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \left\| a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) - \left(b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) \right\| ^ {2} \right] \\ \leq \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \| a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} + \| b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \right] \\ \leq \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(2 a _ {i} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i} + \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \| b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \| ^ {2} \right]\right) \\ \stackrel {(6 3)} {\leq} \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(4 a _ {i} d _ {f _ {i}} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + 2 a _ {i} l \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} + b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i} + \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \| b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \right]\right) \\ \leq 8 \max _ {i} \{a _ {i} \} (f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})) + 4 \max _ {i} \{a _ {i} \} l v _ {k} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i} + {\bf e} _ {k} \left[ \| b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \right]\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

taking the full expectation, we arrive at

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \right] \leq 8 \max  _ {i} \left\{a _ {i} \right\} \mathbf {e} \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 4 \max  _ {i} \left\{a _ {i} \right\} l \mathbf {e} v _ {k} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(b _ {i} \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i} + \mathbf {e} \| b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \| ^ {2}\right). \tag {64}
$$

next, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} g _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \right] = \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} a _ {i} ^ {k} - b _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \right] \\ = \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \right\| ^ {2} \right] \\ = \quad \operatorname {v a r} \left[ \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] + \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right\| ^ {2} \\ \leq \quad \operatorname {v a r} \left[ \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \right] + \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\| \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \right\| ^ {2} \\ \leq \quad \operatorname {v a r} \left[ \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] + \frac {2 l}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) \\ = \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \operatorname {v a r} \left[ a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] + \frac {2 l}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) \\ \leq \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \left\| a _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right\| ^ {2} \right] + \frac {2 l}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) \\ \leq \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(2 a _ {i} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i}\right) + \frac {2 l}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) \\ \leq \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(2 \left(\max  _ {i} \left\{a _ {i} \right\} + n l\right) d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i}\right) \\ \stackrel {(6 3)} {\leq} \left(\frac {4 \max _ {i} \{a _ {i} \}}{n} + 2 l\right) d _ {f} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(2 \left(\max _ {i} \{a _ {i} \} l + n l ^ {2}\right) \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} + b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i}\right) \\ = \left(\frac {4 \max _ {i} \{a _ {i} \}}{n} + 2 l\right) \left(f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})\right) + 2 \left(\frac {\max _ {i} \{a _ {i} \} l}{n} + l ^ {2}\right) v _ {k} + \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} + d _ {1, i}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

further, we define

$$
\omega_ {k} ^ {2} \stackrel {\text {d e f}} {=} \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} \sigma_ {i, k} ^ {2} \tag {65}
$$

and consequently, we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \omega_ {k + 1} ^ {2} \right] = \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} \mathbf {e} \left[ \sigma_ {i, k + 1} ^ {2} \right] \\ \leq \quad (1 - \rho) \omega_ {k} ^ {2} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} c _ {i} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} d _ {2, i} \\ \stackrel {(6 3)} {\leq} \quad (1 - \rho) \omega_ {k} ^ {2} + \frac {4}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} c _ {i} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} c _ {i} l \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} d _ {2, i} \\ \leq (1 - \rho) \omega_ {k} ^ {2} + 4 \max _ {i} \{b _ {i} c _ {i} \} d _ {f} \left(x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + 2 \max _ {i} \{b _ {i} c _ {i} \} l v _ {k} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} d _ {2, i}. \\ \end{array}
$$

we will provide a bound on  $\mathbf{e}||b_i^k -\nabla f_i(x^*)||^2$  based on the choices of  $b_{i}^{k}$  ..

case i. the choice  $b_{i}^{k} = 0$  yields  $\mathbf{e}\|b_{i}^{k} - \nabla f_{i}(x^{*})\|^{2} = \| \nabla f_{i}(x^{*})\|^{2}$ .

case ii. the choice  $b_i^k = \nabla f_i(x^*)$  yields  $\mathbf{e} \| b_i^k - \nabla f_i(x^*) \|^2 = 0$ . overall, for both case i and ii we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \sigma_ {k + 1} ^ {2} \leq (1 - \rho) \mathbf {e} \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} + 4 \max _ {i} \{b _ {i} c _ {i} \} d _ {f} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + 2 \max _ {i} \{b _ {i} c _ {i} \} l v _ {k} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} b _ {i} d _ {2, i}
$$

as desired, where  $\sigma_{k} = \omega_{k}$

case iii. the choice  $b_{i}^{k} = h_{i}^{k} - \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i = 1}^{n}h_{i}^{k}$  yields

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| b _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} = \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left\| h _ {i} ^ {k} - \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} h _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \right\| ^ {2} \leq \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| h _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2}
$$

where

$$
\begin{array}{l} {\mathbf {e} _ {k} \left[ \| h _ {i} ^ {k + 1} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \right]} = {(1 - \rho_ {i} ^ {\prime}) \| h _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} + \rho_ {i} ^ {\prime} \mathbf {e} _ {k} \| l _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2}} \\ \stackrel {(1 6)} {\leq} (1 - \rho_ {i} ^ {\prime}) \| h _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} + 2 \rho_ {i} ^ {\prime} a _ {i} ^ {\prime} d _ {f _ {i}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + \rho_ {i} ^ {\prime} d _ {3, i}. \\ \end{array}
$$

next, set  $\sigma_k^2 \stackrel{\mathrm{def}}{=} \omega_k^2 + \| h_i^k - \nabla f_i(x^*) \|^2$  for this case. consequently, we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} {\mathbf {e} _ {k} \sigma_ {k + 1} ^ {2}} \leq {(1 - \rho) \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} + 4 (\max _ {i} \{b _ {i} c _ {i} \} + \max _ {i} \{\rho_ {i} ^ {\prime} a _ {i} ^ {\prime} \}) d _ {f} (x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}) + 2 (\max _ {i} \{b _ {i} c _ {i} \} + \max _ {i} \{\rho_ {i} ^ {\prime} a _ {i} ^ {\prime} \}) l v _ {k}} \\ + \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(2 b _ {i} d _ {2, i} + \rho_ {i} ^ {\prime} d _ {3, i}\right), \\ \end{array}
$$

where  $\rho = \min_{i}\min \{\rho_{i},\rho_{i}^{\prime}\}$

it remains to plug everything back to (8), (9) and (10).

# g special cases: technical details

# g.1 local-sgd

we start with the analysis of local-sgd (see algorithm 1) under different assumptions of stochastic gradients and data similarity.

algorithm 1 local-sgd  
require: learning rate  $\gamma >0$  , initial vector  $x^0\in \mathbb{r}^d$  , communication period  $\tau \geq 1$    
1: for  $k = 0,1,\ldots$  do   
2: for  $i = 1,\dots ,n$  in parallel do   
3: sample  $g_i^k = \nabla f_{\xi_i^k}(x_i^k)$  independently from other nodes   
4: if  $k + 1$  mod  $\tau = 0$  then   
5:  $x_{i}^{k + 1} = x^{k + 1} = \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i = 1}^{n}\left(x_{i}^{k} - \gamma g_{i}^{k}\right)$  ▷ averaging   
6: else   
7:  $x_{i}^{k + 1} = x_{i}^{k} - \gamma g_{i}^{k}$  ▷ local update   
8: end if   
9: end for   
10: end for

# g.1.1 uniformly bounded variance

in this section we assume that  $f_{i}$  has a form of expectation (see (2)) and stochastic gradients  $\nabla f_{\xi_i}(x)$  satisfy

$$
\mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i}} \left[ \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i}} (x) - \nabla f _ {i} (x) \| ^ {2} \right] \leq d _ {1, i}, \quad \forall x \in \mathbb {r} ^ {d}, \forall i \in [ n ]. \tag {66}
$$

we also introduce the average variance  $\sigma^2$  and the parameter of heterogeneity at the solution  $\zeta_{*}^{2}$  in the following way:

$$
\sigma^ {2} = \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {1, i}, \quad \zeta_ {*} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2}.
$$

lemma g.1. assume that functions  $f_{i}$  are convex and  $l$ -smooth for all  $i \in [n]$ . then

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) \| ^ {2} \leq 6 l \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 3 l ^ {2} v _ {k} + 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2} \tag {67}
$$

and

$$
\left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) \right\| ^ {2} \leq 4 l \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 2 l ^ {2} v _ {k}. \tag {68}
$$

proof. first, to show (67) we shall have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} \stackrel {(1 3 6)} {\leq} \frac {3}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} + \frac {3}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \\ + \frac {3}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6), (1 9)} {\leq} \frac {3 l ^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} + \frac {6 l}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2} \\ = 6 l \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 3 l ^ {2} v _ {k} + 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}. \\ \end{array}
$$

next, to establish (68), we have

$$
\begin{array}{l} \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) \right\| ^ {2} = \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(\nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) \right\| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(1 3 6)} {\leq} \quad \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f (x ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {k}) - \nabla f (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6), (1 9)} {\leq} \frac {2 l ^ {2}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| x _ {i} ^ {k} - x ^ {k} \| ^ {2} + \frac {4 l}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) \\ = \quad 4 l \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 2 l ^ {2} v _ {k}. \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/c4c9d9eaa22a07864f9921f7cd0842e6d64d85e68666f4d2b1b77567f5d9eeec.jpg)

lemma g.2. let  $f_{i}$  be convex and  $l$ -smooth for all  $i \in [n]$ . then for all  $k \geq 0$

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \leq 6 l \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 3 l ^ {2} v _ {k} + \sigma^ {2} + 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}, \tag {69}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \leq \sigma^ {2}, \tag {70}
$$

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} g _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \leq 4 l \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 2 l ^ {2} v _ {k} + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n}, \tag {71}
$$

where  $\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x^k ]\stackrel {def}{=}\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x_1^k,\ldots ,x_n^k ]$

proof. first of all, we notice that  $\bar{g}_i^k = \mathbf{e}\left[g_i^k\mid x^k\right] = \nabla f_i(x_i^k)$ . using this we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x _ {i} ^ {k} \right] \quad = \quad \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} \stackrel {(6 6)} {\leq} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {1, i}, \\ \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x _ {i} ^ {k} \right] \quad \stackrel {(1 3 9)} {=} \quad \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} + \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6 6), (6 7)} {\leq} \quad 6 l \left(f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})\right) + 3 l ^ {2} v _ {k} + \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(d _ {1, i} + 3 \| \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2}\right). \\ \end{array}
$$

finally, using independence of  $g_1^k, g_2^k, \ldots, g_n^k$  we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} g _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \quad \stackrel {(1 3 9)} {\leq} \quad \mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \left(g _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right)\right) \right\| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] + \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) \right\| ^ {2} \\ = \quad \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) \| ^ {2} \mid x _ {i} ^ {k} \right] + \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}\right) \right\| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6 6), (6 8)} {\leq} \quad 4 l \left(f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})\right) + 2 l ^ {2} v _ {k} + \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {1, i}. \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/9591dda4ffb8edab6366d4a4745a2a87949686c45ffcf4bdad4e6b7ea3ada4d6.jpg)

# heterogeneous data

applying corollary e.1 and lemmas g.1 and g.2 we get the following result.

theorem g.1. assume that  $f_{i}(x)$  is  $\mu$ -strongly convex and  $l$ -smooth for every  $i \in [n]$ . then local-sgd satisfies assumption e.1 with

$$
\begin{array}{l} \widetilde {a} = 3 l, \quad \widehat {a} = 0, \quad \widetilde {b} = \widehat {b} = 0, \quad \widetilde {f} = 3 l ^ {2}, \quad \widehat {f} = 0, \quad \widetilde {d} _ {1} = 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}, \quad \widehat {d} = \sigma^ {2}, \\ a ^ {\prime} = 2 l, \quad b ^ {\prime} = 0, \quad f ^ {\prime} = 2 l ^ {2}, \quad d _ {1} ^ {\prime} = \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n}, \quad \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \equiv 0, \quad \rho = 1, \quad c = 0, \quad g = 0, \quad d _ {2} = 0, \\ h = 0, \quad d _ {3} = 2 e (\tau - 1) \left(3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2} + \sigma^ {2}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

with  $\gamma$  satisfying

$$
\gamma \leq \min \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {6 e} (\tau - 1) l} \right\}.
$$

and for all  $k\geq 0$

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)\right).
$$

in particular, if  $\mu > 0$  then

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq (1 - \gamma \mu) ^ {k} \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)\right) \tag {72}
$$

and when  $\mu = 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)\right). \tag {73}
$$

the theorem above together with lemma i.2 implies the following result.

corollary g.1. let assumptions of theorem g.1 hold with  $\mu > 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma = \min \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {6 e} (\tau - 1) l}, \frac {\ln \left(\max \left\{2 , \min \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / \sigma^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 4 e l (\tau - 1) \big (\sigma^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2} \big) \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k} \right\}
$$

for all  $k$  such that

either  $\frac{\ln\left(\max\left\{2,\min\left\{\|x^0 - x^*\|^2n\mu^2k^2 / \sigma^2,\|x^0 - x^*\|^2\mu^3k^3 / 4el(\tau - 1)\left(\sigma^2 + 3(\tau - 1)\zeta_*^2\right)\right\}\right)}{k} \leq 1$

$$
o r \quad \min  \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {6 e} (\tau - 1) l} \right\} \leq \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / \sigma^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 4 e l (\tau - 1) \left(\sigma^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k}
$$

we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\tau l \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {\mu}{\tau l} k\right) + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu k} + \frac {l (\tau - 1) \left(\sigma^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)}{\mu^ {2} k ^ {2}}\right). \tag {74}
$$

that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {\tau l}{\mu} + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu \varepsilon} + \sqrt {\frac {l (\tau - 1) (\sigma^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2})}{\mu^ {2} \varepsilon}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

now we consider some special cases. first of all, if  $d_{1,i} = 0$  for all  $i \in [n]$ , i.e.  $g_i^k = \nabla f_i(x_i^k)$  almost surely, then our result implies that for local-sgd it is enough to perform

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {\tau l}{\mu} + \sqrt {\frac {l (\tau - 1) ^ {2} \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}}{\mu^ {2} \varepsilon}}\right)
$$

iterations in order to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$ . it is clear that for this scenario the optimal choice for  $\tau$  is  $\tau = 1$  which recovers<sup>19</sup> the rate of gradient descent.

secondly, if  $\tau = 1$  then we recover the rate of parallel sgd:

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {l}{\mu} + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu \varepsilon}\right) \qquad \mathrm {c o m m u n i c a t i o n r o u n d s / o r a c l e c a l l s p e r n o d e}
$$

in order to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$ .

finally, our result gives a negative answer to the following question: is local-sgd always worse than parallel minibatch sgd (pmsgd) for heterogeneous data? to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  local-sgd requires

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {\tau l}{\mu} + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu \varepsilon} + \sqrt {\frac {l (\tau - 1) (\sigma^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2})}{\mu^ {2} \varepsilon}}\right) \quad \mathrm {o r a c l e c a l l s p e r n o d e}.
$$

it means that if  $\frac{\sigma^2}{n\sqrt{l(\tau - 1)(\sigma^2 + (\tau - 1)\zeta_*^2)\varepsilon}} \geq 1$  for given  $\tau > 1$  and  $\varepsilon$  and  $\sigma^2$  are such that the first term in the complexity bound is dominated by other terms, then the second term corresponding to the complexity of pmsgd dominates the third term. informally speaking, if the variance is large or  $\varepsilon$  is small then local-sgd with  $\tau > 1$  has the same complexity bounds as pmsgd.

combining theorem g.1 and lemma i.3 we derive the following result for the convergence of local-sgd in the case when  $\mu = 0$ .

corollary g.2. let assumptions of theorem g.1 hold with  $\mu = 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma = \min \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {6 e} (\tau - 1) l}, \sqrt {\frac {n r _ {0} ^ {2}}{\sigma^ {2} k}}, \sqrt [ 3 ]{\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 e l (\tau - 1) (\sigma^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}) k}} \right\},
$$

where  $r_0 = \| x^0 -x^*\|$  , we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] = \mathcal {o} \left(\frac {\tau l r _ {0} ^ {2}}{k} + \sqrt {\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{n k}} + \frac {\sqrt [ 3 ]{l r _ {0} ^ {4} (\tau - 1) \left(\sigma^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)}}{k ^ {2 / 3}}\right). \tag {75}
$$

that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\mathcal {o} \left(\frac {\tau l r _ {0} ^ {2}}{\varepsilon} + \frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{n \varepsilon^ {2}} + \frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sqrt {l (\tau - 1) (\sigma^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2})}}{\varepsilon^ {3 / 2}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

# homogeneous data

in this case we modify the approach a little bit and apply the following result.

lemma g.3 (lemma 1 from [20]). under the homogeneous data assumption for local-sgd we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \sigma^ {2} \tag {76}
$$

for all  $k\geq 0$

using this we derive the following inequality for the weighted sum of  $v_{k}$ :

$$
2 l \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq 2 l (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \sigma^ {2} \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} = 2 l (\tau - 1) \gamma^ {2} \sigma^ {2} w _ {k}.
$$

together with lemmas g.1 and g.2 and theorem 2.1 it gives the following result.

theorem g.2. assume that  $f(x)$  is  $\mu$ -strongly convex and  $l$ -smooth and  $f_{1} = \ldots = f_{n} = f$ . then local-sgd satisfies assumption 2.3 with

$$
\begin{array}{l} a = 3 l, \quad b = 0, \quad f = 3 l ^ {2}, \quad d _ {1} = \sigma^ {2}, \quad a ^ {\prime} = 2 l, \quad b ^ {\prime} = 0, \quad f ^ {\prime} = 2 l ^ {2}, \quad d _ {1} ^ {\prime} = \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n}, \\ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \equiv 0, \quad \rho = 1, \quad c = 0, \quad g = 0, \quad d _ {2} = 0, \quad h = 0, \quad d _ {3} = (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2} \\ \end{array}
$$

with  $\gamma$  satisfying

$$
\gamma \leq \frac {1}{4 l}.
$$

and for all  $k\geq 0$

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 2 l (\tau - 1) \gamma \sigma^ {2}\right).
$$

inparticular,if  $\mu >0$  then

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq (1 - \gamma \mu) ^ {k} \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 2 l (\tau - 1) \gamma \sigma^ {2}\right) \tag {77}
$$

and when  $\mu = 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 2 l (\tau - 1) \gamma \sigma^ {2}\right). \tag {78}
$$

the theorem above together with lemma i.2 implies the following result.

corollary g.3. let assumptions of theorem g.2 hold with  $\mu > 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma = \min \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \frac {\ln \left(\max \left\{2 , \min \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / \sigma^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 2 l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2} \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k} \right\}
$$

for all  $k$  such that

$$
\begin{array}{l} \text {e i t h e r} \quad \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / \sigma^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 2 l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2} \right\} \right\}\right)}{k} \leq 1 \\ o r \quad \frac {1}{4 l} \leq \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / \sigma^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 2 l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2} \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k} \\ \end{array}
$$

we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(l \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {\mu}{l} k\right) + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu k} + \frac {l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2}}{\mu^ {2} k ^ {2}}\right). \tag {79}
$$

that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {l}{\mu} \ln \left(\frac {l \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\varepsilon}\right) + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu \varepsilon} + \sqrt {\frac {l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2}}{\mu^ {2} \varepsilon}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

it means that if  $\frac{\sigma^2}{n^2l\varepsilon} \geq 1$ ,  $\tau \leq 1 + \frac{\sigma^2}{n^2l\varepsilon}$  and  $\varepsilon$  and  $\sigma^2$  are such that the first term in the complexity bound is dominated by other terms, then the second term corresponding to the complexity of pmsgd dominates the third term. informally speaking, if the variance is large or  $\varepsilon$  is small then local-sgd with  $\tau > 1$  has the same complexity bounds as pmsgd.

combining theorem g.2 and lemma i.3 we derive the following result for the convergence of local-sgd in the case when  $\mu = 0$ .

corollary g.4. let assumptions of theorem g.2 hold with  $\mu = 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma = \min  \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \sqrt {\frac {n r _ {0} ^ {2}}{\sigma^ {2} k}}, \sqrt [ 3 ]{\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2} k}} \right\},
$$

where  $r_0 = \| x^0 -x^*\|$  , we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] = \mathcal {o} \left(\frac {l r _ {0} ^ {2}}{k} + \sqrt {\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{n k}} + \frac {\sqrt [ 3 ]{l r _ {0} ^ {4} (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2}}}{k ^ {2 / 3}}\right). \tag {80}
$$

that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\mathcal {o} \left(\frac {l r _ {0} ^ {2}}{\varepsilon} + \frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{n \varepsilon^ {2}} + \frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sqrt {l (\tau - 1) \sigma^ {2}}}{\varepsilon^ {3 / 2}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

# $\zeta$  -heterogeneous data

in this setup we also use an external result to bound  $\mathbf{e}[v_k]$ .

lemma g.4 (lemma 8 from [50]). if  $f_{1}, f_{2}, \ldots, f_{n}$  are  $\zeta$ -heterogeneous then for local-sgd we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq 3 \tau \gamma^ {2} \sigma^ {2} + 6 \tau^ {2} \gamma^ {2} \zeta^ {2} \tag {81}
$$

for all  $k\geq 0$

using this we derive the following inequality for the weighted sum of  $v_{k}$ :

$$
2 l \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} \mathbf {e} \left[ v _ {k} \right] \leq 6 \tau l \gamma^ {2} \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right) \sum_ {k = 0} ^ {k} w _ {k} = 6 \tau l \gamma^ {2} \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right) w _ {k}.
$$

together with lemmas g.1 and g.2 and theorem 2.1 it gives the following result.

theorem g.3. assume that  $f_{1}, \ldots, f_{n}$  are  $\zeta$ -heterogeneous,  $\mu$ -strongly convex and  $l$ -smooth functions. then local-sgd satisfies assumption 2.3 with

$$
\begin{array}{l} a = 3 l, \quad b = 0, \quad f = 3 l ^ {2}, \quad d _ {1} = \sigma^ {2} + 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}, \quad a ^ {\prime} = 2 l, \quad b ^ {\prime} = 0, \quad f ^ {\prime} = 2 l ^ {2}, \quad d _ {1} ^ {\prime} = \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n}, \\ \sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \equiv 0, \quad \rho = 1, \quad c = 0, \quad g = 0, \quad d _ {2} = 0, \quad h = 0, \quad d _ {3} = 3 \tau \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right) \\ \end{array}
$$

with  $\gamma$  satisfying

$$
\gamma \leq \frac {1}{4 l}.
$$

and for all  $k\geq 0$

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / _ {n} + 6 l \tau \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right)\right).
$$

inparticular,if  $\mu >0$  then

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq (1 - \gamma \mu) ^ {k} \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 6 l \tau \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right)\right) \tag {82}
$$

and when  $\mu = 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} / n + 6 l \tau \gamma \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right)\right). \tag {83}
$$

the theorem above together with lemma i.2 implies the following result.

corollary g.5. let assumptions of theorem g.3 hold with  $\mu > 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma = \min  \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / _ {\sigma^ {2}} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 6 l \tau \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k} \right\}
$$

for all  $k$  such that

$$
\text {e i t h e r} \quad \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / \sigma^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 6 l \tau \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{k} \leq 1
$$

$$
o r \quad \frac {1}{4 l} \leq \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{\| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} n \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / _ {\sigma^ {2}} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 6 l \tau \left(\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k}
$$

we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] = \widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(l \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {\mu}{l} k\right) + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu k} + \frac {l \tau \left(\sigma^ {2} + \tau \zeta^ {2}\right)}{\mu^ {2} k ^ {2}}\right). \tag {84}
$$

that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {l}{\mu} \ln \left(\frac {l \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\varepsilon}\right) + \frac {\sigma^ {2}}{n \mu \varepsilon} + \sqrt {\frac {l \tau (\sigma^ {2} + \tau \zeta^ {2})}{\mu^ {2} \varepsilon}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

combining theorem g.3 and lemma i.3 we derive the following result for the convergence of local-sgd in the case when  $\mu = 0$ .

corollary g.6. let assumptions of theorem g.3 hold with  $\mu = 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma = \min  \left\{\frac {1}{4 l}, \sqrt {\frac {n r _ {0} ^ {2}}{\sigma^ {2} k}}, \sqrt [ 3 ]{\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2}}{6 l \tau (\sigma^ {2} + 2 \tau \zeta^ {2}) k}} \right\},
$$

where  $r_0 = \| x^0 -x^*\|$  , we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] = \mathcal {o} \left(\frac {l r _ {0} ^ {2}}{k} + \sqrt {\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{n k}} + \frac {\sqrt [ 3 ]{l r _ {0} ^ {4} \tau \left(\sigma^ {2} + \tau \zeta^ {2}\right)}}{k ^ {2 / 3}}\right). \tag {85}
$$

that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right] \leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\mathcal {o} \left(\frac {l r _ {0} ^ {2}}{\varepsilon} + \frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma^ {2}}{n \varepsilon^ {2}} + \frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sqrt {l \tau (\sigma^ {2} + \tau \zeta^ {2})}}{\varepsilon^ {3 / 2}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

# g.1.2 expected smoothness and arbitrary sampling

in this section we continue our consideration of local-sgd but now we make another assumption on stochastic gradients  $\nabla f_{\xi_i}(x)$ .

assumption g.1 (expected smoothness). we assume that for all  $i \in [n]$  stochastic gradients  $\nabla f_{\xi_i}(x)$  are unbiased estimators of  $\nabla f_i(x)$  and there exists such constant  $\mathcal{l} > 0$  that  $\forall x, y \in \mathbb{r}^d$

$$
\mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} \sim \mathcal {d} _ {i}} \left[ \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i}} (x) - \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i}} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \| ^ {2} \right] \leq 2 \mathcal {l} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x, x ^ {*}\right) \tag {86}
$$

where  $d_{f_i}(x,y)\stackrel {def}{=}f_i(x) - f_i(y) - \langle \nabla f_i(y),x - y\rangle$

in particular, let us consider the following special case. assume that  $f_{i}(x)$  has a form of finite sum (see (3)) and consider the following stochastic reformulation:

$$
f _ {i} (x) = \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i}} \left[ f _ {\xi_ {i}} (x) \right], \quad f _ {\xi_ {i}} (x) = \frac {1}{m} \sum_ {j = 1} ^ {m} \xi_ {i, j} f _ {i, j} (x), \tag {87}
$$

where  $\mathbf{e}[\xi_{i,j}] = 1$  and  $\mathbf{e}[\xi_{i,j}^2 ] < \infty$ . in this case,  $\mathbf{e}_{\xi_i}[\nabla f_{\xi_i}] = \nabla f_i(x)$ . if each  $f_{i,j}(x)$  is  $l_{i,j}$ -smooth then there exists such  $\mathcal{l} \leq \max_{j\in [m]} l_{i,j}$  that assumption g.1 holds. clearly,  $\mathcal{l}$  depends on the sampling strategy and in some cases one can make  $\mathcal{l}$  much smaller than  $\max_{j\in [m]} l_{i,j}$  via good choice of this strategy. our analysis works for an arbitrary sampling strategy that satisfies assumption g.1.

lemma g.5. let  $f_{i}$  be convex and  $l$ -smooth for all  $i \in [n]$ . then for all  $k \geq 0$

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \leq 8 \mathcal {l} \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 4 \mathcal {l} l v _ {k} + 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 2 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}, \tag {88}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \leq 8 \mathcal {l} \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 4 \mathcal {l} l v _ {k} + 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}, \tag {89}
$$

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} g _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \leq 4 (2 \mathcal {l} / n + l) (f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})) + 2 l (2 \mathcal {l} / n + l) v _ {k} + \frac {2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}}{n}, \tag {90}
$$

where  $\sigma_{*}^{2} = \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i = 1}^{n}\mathbf{e}\| \nabla f_{\xi_{i}}(x^{*}) - \nabla f_{i}(x^{*})\|^{2},\zeta_{*}^{2} = \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i = 1}^{n}\| \nabla f_{i}(x^{*})\|^{2}$  and  $\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x^k ]\stackrel {def}{=}\mathbf{e}[\cdot \mid x_1^k,\ldots ,x_n^k ]$

proof. first of all, we notice that  $\bar{g}_i^k = \mathbf{e}\left[g_i^k\mid x^k\right] = \nabla f_i(x_i^k)$ . using this we get

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \stackrel {(1 3 6)} {\leq} \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(8 6), (1 3 9)} {\leq} \quad \frac {4 \mathcal {l}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i}} \left[ \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i}} \left(x ^ {*}\right) - \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \| ^ {2} \right] + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \| \nabla f _ {i} \left(x ^ {*}\right) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6 3)} {\leq} \quad 8 \mathcal {l} \left(f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})\right) + 4 \mathcal {l} l v _ {k} + 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 2 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2} \\ \end{array}
$$

and

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} \left[ \| g _ {i} ^ {k} - \bar {g} _ {i} ^ {k} \| ^ {2} \mid x ^ {k} \right] \quad = \quad \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(1 3 9)} {\leq} \quad \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(1 3 6)} {\leq} \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} + \frac {2}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x ^ {*}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x ^ {*}) \| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(8 6)} {\leq} \quad \frac {4 \mathcal {l}}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} d _ {f _ {i}} \left(x _ {i} ^ {k}, x ^ {*}\right) + 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {(6 3)} {\leq} \quad 8 \mathcal {l} \left(f \left(x ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right)\right) + 4 \mathcal {l} l v _ {k} + 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}. \tag {91} \\ \end{array}
$$

finally, using independence of  $\xi_1^k,\xi_2^k,\ldots ,\xi_n^k$  we obtain

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {e} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} g _ {i} ^ {k} \right\| ^ {2} | x ^ {k} \right] \stackrel {(1 3 9)} {=} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \left[ \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} (\nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k})) \right\| ^ {2} \right] + \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \right\| ^ {2} \\ = \quad \frac {1}{n ^ {2}} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \mathbf {e} _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} \left[ \| \nabla f _ {\xi_ {i} ^ {k}} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) - \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \| ^ {2} \right] + \left\| \frac {1}{n} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {n} \nabla f _ {i} (x _ {i} ^ {k}) \right\| ^ {2} \\ \stackrel {{(9 1), (6 8)}} {{\leq}} \quad 4 \left({^ {2 \mathcal {l}} / n + l}\right) \left(f (x ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*})\right) + 2 l \left({^ {2 \mathcal {l}} / n + l}\right) v _ {k} + \frac {2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}}{n}. \\ \end{array}
$$

![](images/e95d39c83b01986d11008ebda63eadaa5272237c36e64146d0310f0a365c8235.jpg)

# heterogeneous data

applying corollary e.1 and lemmas g.1 and g.5 we get the following result.

theorem g.4. assume that  $f_{i}(x)$  is  $\mu$ -strongly convex and  $l$ -smooth for  $i \in [n]$ . let assumption g.1 holds. then local-sgd satisfies assumption e.1 with

$$
\widetilde {a} = 3 l, \quad \widehat {a} = 4 \mathcal {l}, \quad \widetilde {b} = \widehat {b} = 0, \quad \widetilde {f} = 3 l ^ {2}, \quad \widehat {f} = 4 \mathcal {l} l, \quad \widetilde {d} _ {1} = 3 \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}, \quad \widehat {d} _ {1} = 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}
$$

$$
a ^ {\prime} = \frac {4 \mathcal {l}}{n} + 2 l, \quad b ^ {\prime} = 0, \quad f ^ {\prime} = \frac {4 \mathcal {l} l}{n} + 2 l ^ {2}, \quad d _ {1} ^ {\prime} = \frac {2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}}{n},
$$

$$
\sigma_ {k} ^ {2} \equiv 0, \quad \rho = 1, \quad c = 0, \quad g = 0, \quad d _ {2} = 0,
$$

$$
h = 0, \quad d _ {3} = 2 e (\tau - 1) \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)
$$

with  $\gamma$  satisfying

$$
\gamma \leq \min  \left\{\frac {1}{8 \mathcal {l} / n + 4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {2 e l (\tau - 1) (3 l (\tau - 1) + 4 \mathcal {l})}} \right\}.
$$

and for all  $k\geq 0$

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\overline {{x}} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma w _ {k}} + 2 \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} / n + 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)\right).
$$

in particular, if  $\mu > 0$  then

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq (1 - \gamma \mu) ^ {k} \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma} + 2 \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} / n + 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)\right) \tag {92}
$$

and when  $\mu = 0$  we have

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f \left(\bar {x} ^ {k}\right) - f \left(x ^ {*}\right) \right] \leq \frac {2 \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2}}{\gamma k} + 2 \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} / n + 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)\right). \tag {93}
$$

the theorem above together with lemma i.2 implies the following result.

corollary g.7. let assumptions of theorem g.4 hold with  $\mu > 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma_ {0} = \min  \left\{\frac {1}{8 \mathcal {l} / n + 4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {2 e l (\tau - 1) (3 l (\tau - 1) + 4 \mathcal {l})}} \right\},
$$

$$
\gamma = \min \left\{\gamma_ {0}, \frac {\ln \left(\max \left\{2 , \min \left\{n \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k} \right\}
$$

for all  $k$  such that

$$
\text {e i t h e r} \quad \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{n \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{k} \leq 1
$$

$$
o r \quad \gamma_ {0} \leq \frac {\ln \left(\max  \left\{2 , \min  \left\{n \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {2} k ^ {2} / 2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} , \| x ^ {0} - x ^ {*} \| ^ {2} \mu^ {3} k ^ {3} / 4 e l (\tau - 1) \gamma \left(2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right) \right\} \right\}\right)}{\mu k}
$$

we have that  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right]$  is of the order

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\left(l \tau + \mathcal {l} / n + \sqrt {(\tau - 1) \mathcal {l} l}\right) r _ {0} ^ {2} \exp \left(- \frac {\mu}{l \tau + \mathcal {l} / n + \sqrt {(\tau - 1) \mathcal {l} l}} k\right) + \frac {\sigma_ {*} ^ {2}}{n \mu k} + \frac {l (\tau - 1) \left(\sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)}{\mu^ {2} k ^ {2}}\right),
$$

where  $r_0 = \| x^0 -x^*\|$  . that is, to achieve  $\mathbf{e}\left[f(\overline{x}^k) - f(x^*)\right]\leq \varepsilon$  in this case local-sgd requires

$$
\widetilde {\mathcal {o}} \left(\frac {l \tau}{\mu} + \frac {\mathcal {l}}{n \mu} + \frac {\sqrt {(\tau - 1) \mathcal {l} l}}{\mu} + \frac {\sigma_ {*} ^ {2}}{n \mu \varepsilon} + \sqrt {\frac {l (\tau - 1) (\sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2})}{\mu^ {2} \varepsilon}}\right)
$$

iterations/oracle calls per node and  $\tau$  times less communication rounds.

combining theorem g.4 and lemma i.3 we derive the following result for the convergence of local-sgd in the case when  $\mu = 0$ .

corollary g.8. let assumptions of theorem g.4 hold with  $\mu = 0$ . then for

$$
\gamma_ {0} = \min  \left\{\frac {1}{8 \mathcal {l} / n + 4 l}, \frac {1}{4 \sqrt {2 e l (\tau - 1) (3 l (\tau - 1) + 4 \mathcal {l})}} \right\},
$$

$$
\gamma = \min \left\{\gamma_ {0}, \sqrt {\frac {n r _ {0} ^ {2}}{2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} k}}, \sqrt [ 3 ]{\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2}}{4 e l (\tau - 1) (2 \sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + 3 (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}) k}} \right\},
$$

where  $r_0 = \| x^0 -x^*\|$  , we have that

$$
\mathbf {e} \left[ f (\bar {x} ^ {k}) - f (x ^ {*}) \right] = \mathcal {o} \left(\frac {\left(l \tau + \mathscr {l} / n + \sqrt {(\tau - 1) \mathscr {l} l}\right) r _ {0} ^ {2}}{k} + \sqrt {\frac {r _ {0} ^ {2} \sigma_ {*} ^ {2}}{n k}} + \frac {\sqrt [ 3 ]{l r _ {0} ^ {4} (\tau - 1) \left(\sigma_ {*} ^ {2} + (\tau - 1) \zeta_ {*} ^ {2}\right)}}{k ^ {2 / 3}}\right). \tag {94}
$$
